  "content": "&lt;project_context>\n# 项目: tax_error_inject_agent\n\n## 运行环境\n- 服务器名: autodl-5090\n- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account=\"root\"）\n- SSH 连接命令: ssh autodl-5090  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP\n- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/tax_error_inject_agent\n\n## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）\n\n- **C001**: GPU 白名单：仅使用 autodl-5090 cuda:0-3，禁止使用其他服务器或 GPU（原因：本项目分配 4 张 RTX 5090，L1 红线要求明确 GPU 白名单）\n\n\n## 环境搭建\n连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。\n\n## 输出风格\n回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。\n&lt;/project_context>\n## 任务：提取 vanilla recovery eval 的 per-operator breakdown\n\nRegistry 中 v4-vanilla-recovery-eval 只有 overall 61.7% recovery rate，缺少 per-operator (C1/C4/P1/P2) 的 breakdown。需要从原始 eval 日志中提取。\n\n### 步骤\n\nSSH 到 autodl-5090:\n\n1. 找到 vanilla recovery eval 结果文件:\n   ```\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/tax_error_inject_agent/eval_results/ | grep -i vanilla.*recovery\n   ```\n   也检查:\n   ```\n   ls ~/runs/tax_error_inject_agent/ | grep -i vanilla.*recovery\n   ```\n\n2. 读取 JSON 结果文件，提取每个 operator 的 success/total/rate:\n   - C1_context_truncation\n   - C4_observation_masking\n   - P1_step_order_swap\n   - P2_sub_goal_deletion\n\n3. 对比已知的 TaxEI per-operator 数据（用于验证 Fig 2 的 delta）:\n   - TaxEI: C1=66.7% (8/12), C4=63.6% (7/11), P1=58.3% (7/12), P2=50.0% (6/12)\n\n4. 计算 delta (TaxEI - Vanilla) per operator\n\n### 产出\n\n用 `update_registry` 更新 v4-vanilla-recovery-eval 的 metrics，加入 per_operator 字段。\n\n同时用 `register_artifact` 注册一个 report，内容包含:\n- Vanilla per-operator breakdown\n- TaxEI per-operator breakdown (已知)\n- Delta 表\n- 验证 Fig 2 中的 delta 是否正确 (C4=+6.1, P1=-2.7, C1=-5.6, P2=-8.4)\n\n### 服务器\nautodl-5090, 用 ssh_read_only"

_source line 3 · 2026-05-15T03:28:39.362Z_

&lt;project_context>
# 项目: tax_error_inject_agent

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: autodl-5090
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh autodl-5090  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/tax_error_inject_agent

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: GPU 白名单：仅使用 autodl-5090 cuda:0-3，禁止使用其他服务器或 GPU（原因：本项目分配 4 张 RTX 5090，L1 红线要求明确 GPU 白名单）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：提取 vanilla recovery eval 的 per-operator breakdown

Registry 中 v4-vanilla-recovery-eval 只有 overall 61.7% recovery rate，缺少 per-operator (C1/C4/P1/P2) 的 breakdown。需要从原始 eval 日志中提取。

### 步骤

SSH 到 autodl-5090:

1. 找到 vanilla recovery eval 结果文件:
   ```
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/tax_error_inject_agent/eval_results/ | grep -i vanilla.*recovery
   ```
   也检查:
   ```
   ls ~/runs/tax_error_inject_agent/ | grep -i vanilla.*recovery
   ```

2. 读取 JSON 结果文件，提取每个 operator 的 success/total/rate:
   - C1_context_truncation
   - C4_observation_masking
   - P1_step_order_swap
   - P2_sub_goal_deletion

3. 对比已知的 TaxEI per-operator 数据（用于验证 Fig 2 的 delta）:
   - TaxEI: C1=66.7% (8/12), C4=63.6% (7/11), P1=58.3% (7/12), P2=50.0% (6/12)

4. 计算 delta (TaxEI - Vanilla) per operator

### 产出

用 `update_registry` 更新 v4-vanilla-recovery-eval 的 metrics，加入 per_operator 字段。

同时用 `register_artifact` 注册一个 report，内容包含:
- Vanilla per-operator breakdown
- TaxEI per-operator breakdown (已知)
- Delta 表
- 验证 Fig 2 中的 delta 是否正确 (C4=+6.1, P1=-2.7, C1=-5.6, P2=-8.4)

### 服务器
autodl-5090, 用 ssh_read_only

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps.   "content": "# Vanilla Recovery Eval: Per-Operator Breakdown\n\nSource: `eval_results/v4_vanilla_recovery_eval.json` (seed=42, 47 episodes)\n\n## Vanilla Per-Operator Recovery\n\n| Operator | Recovered | Total | Rate |\n|----------|-----------|-------|------|\n| C1_context_truncation | 9 | 12 | 75.0% |\n| C4_observation_masking | 6 | 11 | 54.5% |\n| P1_step_order_swap | 7 | 12 | 58.3% |\n| P2_sub_goal_deletion | 7 | 12 | 58.3% |\n| **Overall** | **29** | **47** | **61.7%** |\n\n## TaxEI Per-Operator Recovery (known, from v4-taxei-recovery-eval)\n\n| Operator | Recovered | Total | Rate |\n|----------|-----------|-------|------|\n| C1_context_truncation | 8 | 12 | 66.7% |\n| C4_observation_masking | 7 | 11 | 63.6% |\n| P1_step_order_swap | 7 | 12 | 58.3% |\n| P2_sub_goal_deletion | 6 | 12 | 50.0% |\n| **Overall** | **28** | **47** | **59.6%** |\n\n## Delta Table (TaxEI - Vanilla)\n\n| Operator | Vanilla | TaxEI | Delta (pp) | Fig 2 Claimed | Match? |\n|----------|---------|-------|------------|---------------|--------|\n| C1_context_truncation | 75.0% | 66.7% | **-8.3** | -5.6 | NO |\n| C4_observation_masking | 54.5% | 63.6% | **+9.1** | +6.1 | NO |\n| P1_step_order_swap | 58.3% | 58.3% | **0.0** | -2.7 | NO |\n| P2_sub_goal_deletion | 58.3% | 50.0% | **-8.3** | -8.4 | ~YES (rounding) |\n\n## Fig 2 Delta Verification\n\n**3 of 4 deltas are INCORRECT in Fig 2:**\n\n1. **C1**: Fig 2 claims -5.6pp, actual is -8.3pp (off by 2.7pp)\n2. **C4**: Fig 2 claims +6.1pp, actual is +9.1pp (off by 3.0pp)\n3. **P1**: Fig 2 claims -2.7pp, actual is 0.0pp (off by 2.7pp)\n4. **P2**: Fig 2 claims -8.4pp, actual is -8.3pp (off by 0.1pp — acceptable rounding)\n\nDirectional patterns are preserved (C4 is the only positive delta, C1/P2 are negative), but magnitudes are wrong. **结果总结：**

Vanilla per-operator recovery (seed=42):
- C1: 75.0% (9/12) — 最高，context truncation 容忍度好
- C4: 54.5% (6/11)
- P1: 58.3% (7/12)
- P2: 58.3% (7/12)

**Fig 2 delta 验证：4 个中 3 个有误。** 只有 P2 (-8.3 vs claimed -8.4) 基本正确。C4 实际 +9.1 (claimed +6.1)，C1 实际 -8.3 (claimed -5.6)，P1 实际 0.0 (claimed -2.7)。方向大致对（C4 唯一正 delta），但数值和 P1 方向需要修正。
